# Skills · G · part 18

834 entries, gdoc-edit to gear-design. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **gdoc-edit**: Claude Code only. Use when the user wants to change an EXISTING Google Doc in place rather than create a new one: "update the doc", "edit my Google Doc", "add a changelog to the top", "change X to Y in the doc", "insert a section after …", "remove the stale section", "append this to the doc", "what are the comments on this doc", "comment on this doc", "add a comment on X", "leave this feedback as comments", "don't make a new version — edit this one". The complement to gdoc-build (which creates new branded docs). NSLS or Society. ([thensls/nsls-builder-toolkit](https://github.com/thensls/nsls-builder-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/gdoc-edit))
